yathā vā vidagdhamādhave 
mayā te nirbandhānmurajayini rāgaḥ parihṛtya
mayi snigdhe kintu prathaya paramāśīstatimimām |
mukhāmododgāragrahilamatiradyaiva hi yataḥ
pradoṣārambhe syāṃ vimalavanamālāmadhukarī || 14.141 ||
pūrvapūrvastu yo bhāvaḥ somābhādau sa rājate |
tathā bhīṣmasutādau ca śrīharermahiṣīgaṇe || 14.142 ||
ya uttarottaro dviyo rādhikādau sa dīvyati |
tathā śrīsatyabhāmāyāṃ lakṣmaṇāyāmapi kvacit || 14.143 ||
itthaṃ bhedena bhāvānāṃ sarvagokulasubhruvām |
ātmapakṣavipakṣādibhedāḥ pūrvamudīritāḥ || 14.144 ||
yā bhāvāntarasambandhājjāyante vividhā bhidāḥ |
aparā api bhāvānāṃ jñeyāstāḥ prajñayā budhaiḥ || 14.145 ||
atha anurāgaḥ
sadānubhūtamapi yaḥ kuryānnavanavaṃ priyam |
rāgo bhavannavanavaḥ so'nurāga itīryate || 14.146 ||
yathā dānakelikaumudyām 
prapannaḥ panthānaṃ harirasakṛdasmannayanayor
apūrvo'yaṃ pūrvaṃ kvacidapi na dṛṣṭo madhurimā |
pratīke'pyekasya sphurati muhuraṅgasya sakhi yā
śriyastasyāḥ pātuṃ lavamapi samarthā na dṛgiyam || 14.147 ||
yathā vā
ko'yaṃ kṛṣṇa iti vyudasyati dhṛti yastanvi karṇaṃ viśan
rāgāndhe kimidaṃ sadaiva bhavatī tasyorasi krīḍati |
hāsyaṃ mā kuru mohite tvamadhunā nyastāsya haste mayā
satyaṃ satyamasau dṛgaṅganamagādadyaivaa vidyunnibhaḥ || 14.148 ||
parasparavaśībhāvaḥ premavaicittyakaṃ tathā |
aprāṇinyapi janmāptau lālasābhara unnataḥ |
vipralambhe'sya visphūrtirityādyāḥ syuriha kriyāḥ || 14.149 ||
atra parasparavaśībhāvo, yathā
samārambhaṃ pārasparikavijayāya prathannato
rapūrvā keyaṃ vāmaghadamana saṃrambhalaharī |
manohastī bandhastava yadanayā rāganigaḍai
stvaayāpyasyāḥ premotsavanavaguṇaiścittahariṇaḥ || 14.150 ||
premavaicittyasaṃjñastu vipralambhaḥ sa kathyate || 14.151 ||
aprāṇinyapi janmalālasābharo, yathā dānakelikaumudyām 
tapasyāmahkṣāmodari varayituṃ venuṣu janur
vareṇyaṃ manyethāḥ sakhi tadakhilānāṃ sujanuṣām |
tapahstomenoccairyadiyamurarñkṛtya muralī
murārāterbimbādharamadhurimānaṃ rasayati || 14.152 ||
atha vipralambhe visphūrtir, yathā
brūyāstvaṃ mathurādhvanīna mathurānāthaṃ tamityuccakaiḥ
sandeśaṃ vrajasundarī kamapi te kācinmayā prāhiṇot |
tatra kṣmāpatipattane yadi gataḥ svacchanda gacchādhunā
kiṃ kliṣṭāmapi visphuran diśi diśi kliśnāsi hā me sakhīm || 14.153 ||
atha bhāvaḥ
anurāgaḥ svasaṃvedyadaśāṃ prāpya prakāśitaḥ |
yāvadāśrayavṛttiścedbhāva ityabhidhīyate || 14.154 ||
yathā
rādhāyā bhavataśca cittajatunī svedairvilāpya kramāt
yuñjannadrinikuñjakuñjarapate nirdhūtabhedabhramam |
citrāya svayamanvarañjayadiha brahmāṇḍaharmyodare
bhūyobhirnavarāgahiṅgulabharaiḥ śṛṅgārakāruḥ kṛtī || 14.155 ||
mukundamahiṣīvṛndairapyasāvatidurlabhaḥ |
vrajadevyekasaṃvedyo mahābhāvākhyayocyate || 14.156 ||
varāmṛtasvarūpaśrīḥ svaṃ svarūpaṃ mano nayet || 14.157 ||
sa rūḍhaścādhirūḍhaścetyucyate dvividho budhaiḥ || 14.158 ||
tatra rūḍhaḥ
uddīptā sāttvikā yatra sa rūḍha iti bhaṇyate || 14.159 ||
nimeṣāsahatāsannajanatāhṛdviloḍanam |
kalpakṣaṇatvaṃ khinnatvaṃ tatsaukhye'pyārtiśaṅkayā || 14.161 ||
mohādyabhāve'pyātmādisarvavismaraṇaṃ sadā |
kṣaṇasya kalpaetyādyā yatra yogaviyogayoḥ || 14.162 ||
tatra nimeṣāsahatā, yathā śrīdaśame
gopyaśca kṛṣṇamupalabhya cirādabhīṣṭaṃ
yatprekṣaṇe dṛśiṣu pakṣmakṛtaṃ śapanti |
dṛgbhirhṛdīkṛtamalaṃ parirabhya sarvās
tadbhāvamāpurapi nityayujāṃ durāpam || 14.163 ||
āsannajanatāhṛdviloḍanaṃ, yathā
sakhyaḥ prokṣya kurūn gurukṣitibhṛtāmāghūrṇayantī śiraḥ
svasthā viślathayantyaśeṣaramaṇīrāplāvya sarvaṃ janam |
gopīnāmanurāgasindhulaharī satyāntaraṃ vikramai
rākramya stimitāṃ vyadhādapi parāṃ vaikuṇṭhakaṇṭhaśriyam || 14.164 ||
kalpakṣaṇatvam, yathā
śarajjyotsnī rāse vidhirajanirūpāpi nimiṣā
datikṣudrā tāsāṃ yadajani na tadvismayapadam |
sukhotsekārambhe nimiṣalavakalpāmiva daśāṃ
mahākalpākalpāpyahaha labhate kālakalanā || 14.165 ||
tatsaukhye'pyārtiśaṅkayā khinnatvam, yathā śrīdaśame
yatte sujātacaraṇāmburuhaṃ staneṣu̍
bhītāḥ śanaiḥ priya dadhīmahi karkaśeṣu
tenāṭavīmaṭasi tadvyathate na kiṃ svit̍
kūrpādibhirbhramati dhīrbhavadāyuṣāṃ naḥ || 14.166 ||
mohādyabhāve'pi sarvavismaraṇaṃ, yathā ekādaśe
tā nāvidanmayyanuṣaṅgabaddha
dhiyaḥ svamātmānamadastathedam
yathā samādhau munayo'bdhitoye
nadyaḥ praviṣṭā iva nāmarūpe || 14.167 ||
kṣaṇakalpatā, yathā tatraiva
tāstāḥ kṣapāḥ preṣṭhatamena nītā
mayaiva vṛndāvanagocareṇa |
kṣaṇārdhavattāḥ punaraṅga tāsāṃ
hīnā mayā kalpasamā babhūvuḥ || 14.168 ||
ādyaśabdādiha proktā kṛṣṇāvirbhāvakāritā |
sambhogabhede vispaṣṭaṃ sā purastātpravakṣyate || 14.169 ||
atha adhirūḍhaḥ
rūḍhoktebhyo'nubhāvebhyaḥ kāmapyāptā viśiṣṭatām |
yatrānubhāvā dṛśyante so'dhirūḍho nigadyate || 14.170 ||
yathā śivavākyam
lokātīatamajāṇḍakoṭigamapi traikālikaṃ yatsukhaṃ
duḥkhaṃ ceti pṛthagyadi sphuṭamubhe te gacchataḥ kuṭatām |
naivābhāsatulāṃ śive tadapi tatkuṭadvayaṃ rādhikā
premodyatsukhaduḥkhasindhubhavayorvindeta bindvorapi || 14.171 ||
modano mādanaścāsāvadhirūḍho dvidhocyate || 14.172 ||
tatra modanaḥ
modanaḥ sa dvayoryatra sāttvikoddīptasauṣṭhavam || 14.173 ||
yathā lalitamādhave 
ātanvan kalakaṇṭhanādamatulaṃ stambhaśriyojjṛmbhito
bhūyiṣṭhocchaladaṅkuraḥ phalitavān svedāmbumuktāphalaiḥ |
udyadbāṣpamarandabhāgavicalo'pyutkampavān vibhramai
rādhāmādhavayorvirājati cirādullāsakalpadrumaḥ || 14.174 ||
hareryatra sakāntasya vikṣobhabharakāritā |
premorusampadvikhyātakāntātiśayitādayaḥ || 14.175 ||
rādhikāyūtha evāsau modano na tu sarvataḥ |
yaḥ śrīmān hlādinīśakteḥ suvilāsaḥ priyo varaḥ || 14.176 ||
tatra sakāntasya hareḥ kṣobhabharakāritā, yathā
hanta stambhakarambitā bhuvi kurorbhadrā sarasvatyabhū
dbāṣpaṃ bhāskarajā mumoca tarasā satyābhramannarmadā |
bheje bhīṣmasutā ca varṇavikṛtiṃ gāmbhīryabhāgapyasau
kṛṣṇodanvati rādhikādbhutanadīpremormibhiḥ saṃvṛte || 14.177 ||
premorusampadvatīvṛndātiśayitvaṃ, yathā
advaitādgirijāṃ harārdhavapuṣaṃ sakhyātpriyoraḥsthitāṃ
lakṣmīmacyutacittabhṛṅganalinīṃ satyāṃ ca saubhāgyataḥ |
mādhuryānmadhureśajīvitasakhīṃ candrāvalīṃ ca kṣipan
paśyāruddha hariṃ prasārya laharīṃ rādhānurāgāmbudhiḥ || 14.178 ||
modano'yaṃ praviśleṣadaśāyāṃ mohano bhavet |
yasmin virahavaivaśyātsūddīptā eva sāttvikāḥ || 14.179 ||
yathā
udyadvepathuvādyamānadaśanā kaṇṭhasthalāntarluṭha
jjalpā gokulamaṇḍalaṃ vidadhatī bāṣpairnadīmātṛkam |
rādhā kaṇṭakitena kaṇṭakiphalaṃ gātreṇa dhikkurvatī
citraṃ tadghanarāgarāśibhirapi śvetīkṛtā vartate || 14.180 ||
atrānubhāvā govinda kāntāśliṣṭe'pi mūrcchanā |
asahyaduḥkhasvīkārādapi tatsukhakāmatā || 14.181 ||
brahmāṇḍakṣobhakāritvaṃ tiraścāmapi rodanam |
svabhūtairapi tatsaṅgatṛṣṇā mṛtyupratiśravāt |
divyonmādādayo'pyanye vidvadbhiranukīrtitāḥ || 14.182 ||
prāyo vṛndāvaneśvaryāṃ mohano'yamudañcati |
samyagvilakṣaṇaṃ yasya kāryaṃ sañcārimohataḥ || 14.183 ||
tatra kāntāśliṣṭe'pi harau mūrcchākāritvaṃ, yathā padyāvalyām 
ratnacchāyācchuritajaladhau mandire dvārakāyā
rukmiṇyāpi prabalapulakodbhedamāliṅgitasya |
viśvaṃ pāyānmasṛṇayamunātīravānīrakuñje
rādhākelīparimalabharadhyānamūrcchā murāreḥ || 14.184 ||
asahyaduḥkhasvīkārāttatsukhakāmatā
syānnaḥ saukhyaṃ yadapi balavadgoṣṭhamāpte mukunde
yadyalpāpi kṣatirudayate tasya māgātkadāpi |
aprāpte'smin yadapi nagarādārtirugrā bhavennaḥ
saukhyaṃ tasya sphurati hṛdi cettatra vāsaṃ karotu || 14.185 ||
brahmāṇḍakṣobhakāritvaṃ, yathā
nāraṃ cukrośa cakraṃ phaṇikulamabhavadvyākulaṃ svedamūhe
vṛndaṃ vṛndārakāṇāṃ pracuramudamamucannaśru vaikuṇṭhabhājaḥ |
rādhāyāścitramīśa bhramati diśi diśi premaniḥśvāsadhūme
pūrṇānande'pyuṣitvā bahiridamabahiścārtamāsīdajāṇḍam || 14.186 ||
yathā vā
aurvastomātkaṭurapi kathaṃ durbalenorasā me
tāpaḥ prauḍho harivirahajaḥ sahyate tanna jāne |
niṣkrāntā cedbhavati hṛdayādyasya dhūmacchaṭāpi
brahmāṇḍānāṃ sakhi kulamapi jvālayā jājvalīti || 14.187 ||
tiraścāmapi rodanam, yathā padyāvalyām 
yāte dvāravatīpuraṃ muraripau tadvastrasaṃvyānayā
kālindītaṭakuñjavañjulalatāmālāmbya sotkaṇṭhayā |
udgītaṃ gurubāṣpagadgadagalattārasvaraṃ rādhayā
yenāntarjalacāribhirjalacarairapyutkamutkūjitam || 14.188 ||
mṛtyusvīkārātsvabhūtairapi tatsaṅgatṛṣṇā, yathā tatraiva 
pañcatvaṃ tanuretu bhūtanivahāu svāṃśe viśantu sphuṭaṃ
dhātāraṃ praṇipatya hanta śirasā tatrāpi yāce varam |
tadvāpīṣu payastadīyamukure jyotistadīyāṅgana
vyomni vyoma tadīyavartmani dharā tattālavṛnte'nilaḥ || 14.189 ||
atha divyonmādaḥ
etasya mohanākhyasya gatiṃ kāmapyupeyuṣaḥ |
bhramābhā kāpi vaicitrī divyonmāda itīryate || 14.190 ||
udghūrṇācitrajalpādyāstadbhedā bahavo matāḥ || 14.191 ||
tatra udghūrṇā
syādvilakṣaṇamudghūrṇā nānāvaivaśyaceṣṭitam || 14.192 ||
yathā
śayyāṃ kuñjagṛhe kvacidvitanute sā vāsasajjāyitā
nīlābhraṃ dhṛtakhaṇḍitā vyavahṛtiścaṇḍī kvacittarjati |
āghūrṇatyabhisārasambhramavatī dhvānte kvaciddāruṇe
rādhā te virahodbhramapramathitā dhatte na kāṃ vā daśām || 14.193 ||
mathurānagaraṃ kṛṣṇe labdhe lalitamādhave |
udghūrṇeyaṃ tṛtīyāṅke rādhāyāḥ sphuṭamīritā || 14.194 ||
atha citrajalpaḥ
preṣṭhasya suhṛdāloke gūḍharoṣābhijṛmbhitaḥ |
bhūribhāvamayo jalpo yastīvrotkaṇṭhitāntimaḥ || 14.195 ||
citrajalpo daśāṅgo'yaṃ prajalpaḥ parijalpitam |
vijalpojjalpasaṃjalpā avajalpo'bhijalpitam |
ājalpaḥ pratijalpaśca sujalpaśceti kīrtitāḥ || 14.196 ||
eṣa bhramaragītākhyo daśame prakaṭīkṛtaḥ || 14.197 ||
asaṅkhyabhāvavaicitrī camatkṛtisudustaraḥ |
api ceccitrajalpo'yaṃ manāktadapi kathyate || 14.198 ||
tatra prajalpaḥ
asūyerṣyāmadayujā yo'vadhīraṇamudrayā |
priyasyākauśalodgāraḥ prajalpaḥ sa tu kīrtyate || 14.199 ||
yathā
madhupa kitavabandho mā spṛśāṅghriṃ sapatnyāḥ
kucavilulitamālākuṅkumaśmaśrubhirnaḥ |
vahatu madhupatistanmāninīnāṃ prasādaṃ
yadusadasi viḍambyaṃ yasya dūtastvamīdṛk || 14.200 ||
atha parijalpitam
prabhornirdayatāśāṭhyacāpalādyupapādanāt |
svavicakṣaṇatāvyaktirbhaṅgyā syātparijalpitam || 14.201 ||
yathā
sakṛdadharasudhāṃ svāṃ mohinīṃ pāyayitvā
sumanasa iva sadyastatyaje'smān bhavādṛk |
paricarati kathaṃ tatpādapadmaṃ nu padmā
hyapi bata hṛtacetā hyuttamaḥślokajalpaiḥ || 14.202 ||
atha vijalpaḥ
vyaktayāsūyayā gūḍhamānamudrāntarālayā |
aghadviṣi kaṭākṣoktirvijalpo viduṣāṃ mataḥ || 14.203 ||
yathā
kimiha bahu ṣaḍaṅghre gāyasi tvaṃ yadūnām
adhipatimagṛhāṇāmagrato naḥ purāṇam |
vijayasakhasakhīnāṃ gīyatāṃ tatprasaṅgaḥ
kṣapitakucarujaste kalpayantīṣṭamiṣṭāḥ || 14.204 ||
atha ujjalpaḥ
hareḥ kuhakatākhyānaṃ garvagarbhitayerṣyayā |
sāsūyaśca tadākṣepo dhīrairujjalpa īryate || 14.205 ||
yathā
divi bhuvi ca rasāyāṃ kāḥ striyastaddurāpāḥ
kapaṭarucirahāsabhrūvijṛmbhasya yāḥ syuḥ |
caraṇaraja upāste yasya bhūtirvayaṃ kā
api ca kṛpaṇapakṣe hyuttamaḥślokaśabdaḥ || 14.206 ||
atha saṃjalpaḥ
solluṇṭhayā gahanayā kayāpyākṣepamudrayā |
tasyākṛtajñatādyuktiḥ saṃjalpaḥ kathito budhaiḥ || 14.207 ||
yathā
visṛja śirasi pādaṃ vedmyahaṃ cāṭukārair
anunayaviduṣaste'bhyetya dautyairmukundāt |
svakṛta iha visṛṣṭāpatyapatyanyalokā
vyasṛjadakṛtacetāḥ kiṃ nu sandheyamasmin || 14.208 ||
atha avajalpaḥ
harau kāṭhinyakāmitvadhaurtyādāsaktyayogyatā |
yatra serṣyaṃ bhiyevoktā so'vajalpaḥ satāṃ mataḥ || 14.209 ||
yathā
mṛgayuriva kapīndraṃ vivyadhe lubdhadharmā
striyamakṛta virūpāṃ strījitaḥ kāmayānām |
balimapi balimattvāveṣṭayaddhvāṅkṣavadyas
tadalamasitasakhyairdustyajastatkathārthaḥ || 14.210 ||
atha abhijalpitam
bhaṅgyā tyāgaucitī tasya khagānāmapi khedanāt |
yatra sānuśayaṃ proktā tadbhavedabhijalpitam || 14.211 ||
yathā
yadanucaritalīlākarṇapīyūṣavipruṭ
sakṛdadanavidhūtadvandvadharmā vinaṣṭāḥ |
sapadi gṛhakuṭumbaṃ dīnamutsṛjya dīnā
bahava iha vihaṅgā bhikṣucaryāṃ caranti || 14.212 ||
atha ājalpaḥ
jaihmyaṃ tasyārtidatvaṃ ca nirvedādyatra kīrtitam |
bhaṅyānyasukhadatvaṃ ca sa ājalpa udīritaḥ || 14.213 ||
yathā
vayamṛtamiva jihmavyāhṛtaṃ śraddadhānāḥ
kulikarutamivājñāḥ kṛṣṇavadhvo hariṇyaḥ
dadṛśurasakṛdetattannakhasparśatīvra
smararuja upamantrin bhaṇyatāmanyavārtā || 14.214 ||
atha pratijalpaḥ
dustyajadvandvabhāve'smin prāptirnārhetyanuddhatam |
dūtasammānanenoktaṃ yatra sa pratijalpakaḥ || 14.215 ||
yathā
priyasakha punarāgāḥ preyasā preṣitaḥ kiṃ
varaya kimanurundhe mānanīyo'si me'ṅga |
nayasi kathamihāsmān dustyajadvandvapārśvaṃ
satatamurasi saumya śrīrvadhūḥ sākamāste || 14.216 ||
atha sujalpaḥ
yatrārjavātsagāmbhīryaṃ sadainyaṃ sahacāpalam |
sotkaṇṭhaṃ ca hariḥ pṛṣṭaḥ sa sujalpo nigadyate || 14.217 ||
yathā
api bata madhupuryāmāryaputro'dhunāste
smarati sa pitṛgehān saumya bandhūṃśca gopān |
kvacidapi sa kathāṃ naḥ kiṅkarīṇāṃ gṛṇīte
bhujamagurusugandhaṃ mūrdhnyadhāsyatkadā nu || 14.218 ||
atha mādanaḥ
sarvabhāvodgamollāsī mādano'yaṃ parātparaḥ |
rājate hlādinīsāro rādhāyāmeva yaḥ sadā || 14.219 ||
yathā
āsṛṣṭerakṣayiṣṇuṃ hṛdayavidhumaṇidrāvaṇaṃ vakrimāṇaṃ
pūrṇatve'pyudvahantaṃ nijarucighaṭayā sādhvasaṃ dhvaṃsayantam |
tanvānaṃ śaṃ pradoṣe dhṛtanavanavatāsampadaṃ mādanatvā
dadvaitaṃ naumi rādhādanujavijayinoradbhutaṃ bhāvacandram
|| 14.220 ||
atrerṣyāyā ayogye'pi prabalerṣyāvidhāyitā |
sadābhoge'pi tadgandhamātrādhārastavādayaḥ || 14.221 ||
atha ayogye'pīrṣyā, yathā dānakelikaumudyām 
viśuddhābhiḥ sārdhaṃ vrajahariṇanetrābhiraniśaṃ
tvamaddhā vidveṣaṃ kimiti vanamāle racayasi |
tṛṇīkurvatyasmān vapuraghariporāśikhamidaṃ
pariṣvajyāpāda mahati hṛdaye yā viharasi || 14.222 ||
sadābhoge'pi tadgandhamātrādhārastutir, yathā śrīdaśame
pūrṇāḥ pulindya urugāyapadābjarāga
śrīkuṅkumena dayitāstanamaṇḍitena |
taddarśanasmararujastṛṇarūṣitena
limpantya ānanakuceṣu juhustadādhim || 14.223 ||
yathā vā
duṣkaraṃ kataradāli mālatī
komaleyamakarottapaḥ purā |
hanta goṣṭhapatinandanopamaṃ
yā tamālamamalopagūhate || 14.224 ||
yoga eva bhavedeṣa vicitraḥ ko'pi mādanaḥ |
yadvilāsā virājante nityalīlāḥ sahasradhā || 14.225 ||
mādanasya gatiḥ suṣṭhu madanasyeva durgamā |
na nirvaktuṃ bhavecchakyā tenāsau munināpyalam || 14.226 ||
kiṃ ca
rāgānurāgatāmādau snehaḥ prāpyaiva satvaram |
mānatvaṃ praṇayatvaṃ ca kvacitpaścātprapadyate || 14.227 ||
ataevātra śāstreṣu śrūyate rādhikādiṣu |
pūrvarāgaprasaṅge'pi prakaṭaṃ rāgalakṣaṇam || 14.228 ||
sphuranti vrajadevīṣu parā bhāvabhidāśca yāḥ |
tāstarkāygocaratyā na samyagiha varṇitāḥ || 14.229 ||
sādhāraṇyāṃ ratāveva dhūmāyitatayā matāḥ |
jvalitāstu ratipremṇordīptāḥ snehādipañcasu |
rūḍhe bhāve tathoddīptāḥ sudīptā mohanādiṣu || 14.230 ||
iyaṃ prāyikatā kintu śreṣṭhamadhyādibhārataḥ |
deśakālajanādīnāṃ kvāpyeṣāṃ syādviparyayam || 14.231 ||
ādyā premāntimāṃ tatrānurāgāntāṃ samañjasā |
ratirbhāvāntimāṃ sīmāṃ samarthaiva prapadyante || 14.232 ||
ratirnarmavayasyānāmanurāgāntimāṃ sthitim |
teṣveva subalādīnāṃ bhāvāntāmeva gacchati || 14.233 ||
iti śrīśrīujjvalanīlamaṇau
sthāyibhāvaprakaraṇam
||14||

atha śṛṅgārabhedaprakaraṇam
sa vipralambhaḥ sambhoga iti dvedhojjvalo mataḥ || 15.1 ||
tatra vipralambhaḥ
yūnorayuktayorbhāvo yuktayorvā tayormithaḥ |
abhīṣṭāliṅganādīnāmanavāptau prakṛṣyate |
sa vipralambho vijñeyaḥ sambhogonnatikārakaḥ || 15.2 ||
tathā coktam
na vinā vipralambhena sambhogaḥ puṣṭimaśnute |
kāṣāyite hi vastrādau bhūyānevābhivardhate || 15.3 ||
pūrvarāgastathā mānaḥ premavaicittyamityapi |
pravāsaśceti kathito vipralambhaścaturvidhaḥ || 15.4 ||
tatra pūrvarāgaḥ
ratiryā saṅgamātpūrvaṃ darśanaśravaṇādijā |
tayorunmīlati prājñaiḥ pūrvarāgaḥ sa ucyate || 15.5 ||
tatra darśanāt
sākṣātkṛṣṇasya citre ca syātsvapnādau ca darśanam || 15.6 ||
tatra sākṣāt, yathā padyāvalyām 
indīvarodarasahodarameduraśrīr
vāso dravatkanakavṛndanibhaṃ dadhānaḥ |
āmuktamauktikamanoharahāravakṣāḥ
ko'yaṃ yuvā jagadanaṅgamayaṃ karoti || 15.7 ||
citre, yathā vidagdhamādhave 
śiśiraya dṛśau dṛṣṭvā divyaṃ kiśoramitīkṣitaḥ
parijalpanagirāṃ viśrambhāttvaṃ vilāsaphalāṅkitaḥ |
śiva śiva kathaṃ jānīmastvāmavakradhiyo vayaṃ
niviḍabaḍavāvahnijvālākalāpavikāsinam || 15.8 ||
svapne, yathā
svapne dṛṣṭvā sahacari saritkāsarī śyāmanīrā
tīre tasyāḥ kvaṇitamadhupā mādhavaīkuñjaśālā |
tasyāṃ kāntaṃ kapiśajaghano dhvāntarāśiḥ śarīrī
citraṃ candrāvalimapi sa māṃ pātumicchannarautsīt || 15.9 ||
atha śravaṇam
vandidūtīsakhīvaktrādgītādeśca śrutirbhavet || 15.10 ||
tato vandivaktrāt, yathā
paṭhati magadharājanirjayārthāṃ
sakhi virudāvalimatra vandivarye |
vada kathamiva lakṣmaṇe tanute
pulakakulena vilakṣaṇā kilāsīt || 15.11 ||
dūtīvaktrāt, yathā
āviṣkṛte tava mukunda mayā prasaṅge
tārāvalī pulakitāṅgalatā natākṣī |
śuśrūṣurapyalaghugadgadaruddhakaṇṭhī
praṣṭuṃ tavākṣamata sā na kathāviśeṣam || 15.12 ||
sakhīvaktrāt, yathā
yāvadunmadacakoralocanā
manmukhāttava kathāmupāśṛṇot |
tāvadañcati dinaṃ dinaṃ sakhī
kṛṣṇa śāradanadīrya tānavam || 15.13 ||
gītāt, yathā
nayane praṇayannudaśruṇī
mama sadyaḥ sadasi kṣitīśituḥ |
upavīṇayati pravīṇadhīḥ
kamudaśruḥ sakhi vaiṇiko muniḥ || 15.14 ||
puroktā ye'bhiyogādyā hetavo ratijanmani |
atra te pūrvarāge'pi jñeyā dhīrairyathocitam || 15.15 ||
api mādhavarāgasya prāthamye sambhavatyapi |
ādau rāge mṛgākṣīṇāṃ prokte syāccārutādhikā || 15.16 ||
atra sañcāriṇo vyādhiḥ śaṅkāsūyā śramaḥ klamaḥ |
nirvedautsukyadainyāni cintānidrāprabodhanam || 15.17 ||
viṣādo jaḍatonmado mohamṛtyādayaḥ smṛtāḥ |
prauḍhaḥ samañjasaḥ sādhāraṇaśceti sa tu tridhā || 15.18 ||
tatra prauḍhaḥ
samarthaaratirūpastu prauḍha ityabhidhīyate |
lālasādiriha prauḍhe maraṇāntā daśā bhavet |
tattatsañcāribhāvānāmutkaṭatvādanekadhā || 15.19 ||
tathāpi prāktanairasya daśāvasthā samāsataḥ |
proktāstadanurodhena tāsāṃ lakṣaṇamucyate || 15.20 ||
lālasodvegajāgaryāstānavaṃ jaḍimātra tu |
vaiyagryaṃ vyādhirunmādo moho mṛtyurdaśā daśa || 15.21 ||
prauḍhatvātpūrvarāgasya prauḍhāḥ sarvā daśā api || 15.22 ||
tatra lālasaḥ
abhīṣṭalipsayā gāḍhagṛdhnutā lālaso mataḥ |
atrautsukyaṃ capalatā ghūrṇāśvāsādayastathā || 15.23 ||
yathā
tvamavasitānniṣkrāmantī punaḥ praviśantyasau
jhaṭiti ghaṭikāmadhye vārāñchataṃ vrajasīmani |
agaṇitagurutrāsā śvāsān vimucya vimucya kiṃ
kṣipasi bahuśo nīpāraṇye kiśori dṛśordvayam || 15.24 ||
yathā vā vidagdhamādhave 
dūrādapyanuṣaṅgataḥ śrutimite tvannāmadheyākṣare
sonmādaṃ madirekṣaṇā viruvatī dhatte muhurvepathum |
āḥ kiṃ vā kathanīyamanyadasite daivādvarāmbhodhare
dṛṣṭe taṃ parirabdhumutsukamatiḥ pakṣadvayīmicchati || 15.25 ||
atha udvegaḥ
udvego manasaḥ kampastatra niḥśvāsacāpale |
stambhaścintāśruvaivarṇyasvedādaya udīritāḥ || 15.26 ||
yathā vidagdhamādhave 
cintāsantatiradya kṛntati sakhi svāntasya kiṃ te dhṛtiṃ
kiṃvā siñcati tāmramambaramatisvedāmbhasāṃ ḍambaraḥ |
kampaścampakagauri lumpati vapuḥsthairyaṃ kathaṃ vā balāt
tathyaṃ brūhi na maṅgalā parijane saṅgopanāṅgīkṛtiḥ || 15.27 ||
atha jāgaryā
nidrākṣayastu jāgaryā stambhaśoṣagadādikṛt || 15.28 ||
yathā
śyāmaṃ kañcana kāñcanojjvalapaṭaṃ sandarśya nidrā kṣaṇaṃ
māmājanma sakhī vimucya calitā ruṣṭeva nāvartate |
cintāṃ prohya sakhi prapañcaya matiṃ tasyāstvamāvartane
nānyaḥ svāpnikataskaropaharaṇe śakto janastāṃ vinā || 15.29 ||
atha tānavam
tānavaṃ kṛśatā gātre daurbalyabhramaṇādikṛt || 15.30 ||
yathā
cyute valayasañcaye prabalariktatādūṣaṇa
vyayāya nihitormikāvalirapi skhalatyañjasā |
niśamya muralīkalaṃ sakhi sakṛdviśākhe tanu
stavāsitacaturdaśīśaśikalā kṛśatvaṃ yayau || 15.31 ||
kaiścittu tānavasthāne vilāpaḥ paripaṭhyate || 15.32 ||
yathā
atrāsīnnavanīpabhūruhataṭe kurvan vihāraṃ hari
ścakre tāṇḍavamatra mitrasahitaścaṇḍāṃśujārodhasi |
paśyantī latikāntare kṣaṇamahaṃ vyagrā nilīya sthitaṃ
sakhyaḥ kiṃ kathayāmi dagdhavidhinā kṣiptāsmi dāvopari || 15.33 ||
atha jaḍimā
iṣṭāniṣṭāparijñānaṃ yatra praśneṣvanuttaram |
darśanaśravaṇābhāvo jaḍimā so'bhidhīyate |
atrākāṇḍe'pi huṅkārastambhaśvāsabhramādayaḥ || 15.34 ||
yathā
akāṇḍe huṅkāraṃ racayasi śṛṇoṣi priyasakhī
kulānāṃ nālāpaṃ dṛtiriva muhurniḥśvasiṣi ca |
tataḥ śaṅke paṅkeruhamukhi yayau vaiṇavakalā
madhulī te pāli śruticaṣakayoḥ prāghūṇikatām || 15.35 ||
atha vaiyāgryam
vaiyāgryaṃ bhāvagāmbhīryavikṣobhāsahatocyate |
tatrāvivekanirvedakhedāsūyādayo matāḥ || 15.36 ||
yathā vidagdhamādhave 
pratyāhṛtya muniḥ kṣaṇaṃ viṣayato yasminmano dhitsate
bālāsau viṣayeṣu dhitsati tataḥ pratyāharantī manaḥ |
yasya sphūrtilavāya hanta hṛdaye yogī sumutkaṇṭhate
mugdheyaṃ kila tasya paśya hṛdayānniṣkrāntimākāṅkṣati || 15.37 ||
atha vyādhiḥ
abhīṣṭālābhato vyādhiḥ pāṇḍimottāpalakṣaṇaḥ |
atra śītaspṛhāmohaniḥśvāsapatanādayaḥ || 15.38 ||
yathā
davadamanatayā niśamya bhadrā
madanadavajvalitā dadhe hṛdi tvām |
dviguṇitadavathuvyathāvidagdhā
murahara bhasmamayīva pāṇḍurāsīt || 15.39 ||
atha unmādaḥ
sarvāvasthāsu sarvatra tanmanaskatayā sadā |
atasmiṃstu taditi bhrāntirunmāda iti kīrtyate |
atreṣṭadveṣaniḥśvāsanimeṣavirahādayaḥ || 15.40 ||
yathā vidagdhamādhave 
vitanvānastanvā marakatarucīnāṃ rucivatāṃ
paṭānniṣkrānto'bhūddhṛtaśikhaṇḍo navayuvā |
bhruvaṃ tena kṣiptvā kimapi hasatonmāditamateḥ
śaśī vṛtto vahniḥ paramahaha vahnirmama śaśī || 15.41 ||
atha mohaḥ
moho vicittatā prokto naiścalyapatanādikṛt || 15.42 ||
yathā
nāsāśvāsaparāṅmukhī vighaṭite dṛṣṭī snuṣāyāḥ kathaṃ
hā dhikkṛṣṇatilānmamārpaya kare kuryāmapāmārjanam |
ityārohati karṇayoḥ parisaraṃ kṛṣṇeti karṇadvaye
kampenācyuta tatra sūtritavatī tvāmeva hetuṃ sakhī || 15.43 ||
atha mṛtyuḥ
taistaiḥ kṛtaiḥ pratīkārairyadi na syātsamāgamaḥ |
kandarpabāṇakadanāttatra syānmaraṇodyamaḥ || 15.44 ||
tatra svapriyavastūnāṃ vayasyāsu samarpaṇam |
bhṛṅgamandānilajyotsnākadambānubhavādayaḥ || 15.45 ||
yathā
rādhā rodhasi ropitāṃ mukulinīmāliṅgya mallīlatāṃ
hāraṃ hīramayaṃ samarpya lalitāhaste praśastaśriyam |
mūrcchāmāpnuvatī praviśya madhupairgītāṃ kadambāṭavīṃ
nāma vyāharatā hareḥ priyasakhīvṛndena sandhukṣitā || 15.46 ||
yathā vā vidagdhamādhave 
akāruṇyaḥ kṛṣṇo yadi mayi tavāgaḥ kathamidaṃ
mudhā mā rodīrme kuru paramimāmuttarakṛtim |
tamālasya skandhe vinihitabhujavallaririyaṃ
yathā vṛndāraṇye ciramavicalā tiṣṭhati tanuḥ || 15.47 ||
atha samañjasaḥ
bhavetsamañjasaratisvarūpo'yaṃ samañjasaḥ || 15.48 ||
atrābhilāṣacintāsmṛtiguṇasaṅkīrtanodvegāḥ |
savilāpā unmādavyādhijaḍatā mṛtiśca tāḥ kramaśaḥ || 15.49 ||
tatra abhilāṣaḥ
vyavasāyo'bhilāṣaḥ syātpriyasaṅgamalipsayā |
svamaṇḍanāntikaprāptirāgaprakaṭanādikṛt || 15.50 ||
yathā
yadiha sakhi subhadrāsakhyamākhyāya dhūrte
vrajasi pituragārāddevekīmandirāya |
racayasi bata satye maṇḍane ca prayatnaṃ
sphuṭamajani tadantarvastu gūḍhaṃ tavādya || 15.51 ||
atha cintā
abhīṣṭāvāptyupāyānāṃ dhyānaṃ cintā prakīrtitā |
śayyāvivṛttiniḥśvāsanirlakṣaprekṣaṇādikṛt || 15.52 ||
yathā
niḥśvāsaste kamalavadane mlāpayatyoṣṭhabimbaṃ
śayyāyāṃ ca kraśimakalitā ceṣṭate dehayaṣṭiḥ |
dvandvaṃ cākṣṇorvikirati ciraṃ rukmiṇi śyāma ambho
na śvobhāvinyupayamavidhau śobhate vikriyeyam || 15.53 ||
atha smṛtiḥ
anubhūtapriyādīnāmarthānāṃ cintanaṃ smṛtiḥ |
atra kampāṅgavaivaśyabāṣpaniḥśvasitādayaḥ || 15.54 ||
yathā
plutaṃ pureṇāpāṃ nayanakamaladvandvamabhito
dhṛtotkampaṃ sātrājiti kucarathāṅgadvayamapi |
ślathārambhaṃ caitadbhujavisalayugaṃ tattava mana
staḍāge'smin kṛṣṇadviradapatirantarviharati || 15.55 ||
atha guṇakīrtanam
saundaryādiguṇaślāghā guṇakīrtanamucyate |
atra vepathuromāñcakaṇṭhagadgadikādayaḥ || 15.56 ||
yathā
yāntyastṛṣṇāpi yuvatayo yeṣu ghūrṇāṃ bhajante
yānyācamya svayamapi bhavān romaharṣaṃ prayāti |
gandhaṃ teṣāṃ tava madhupate rūpasampanmadhūnāṃ
dūre vindanmama na hi dhṛtiḥ cittabhṛṅgastanoti || 15.57 ||
ṣaḍudvegādayaḥ pūrvaṃ prauḍhe tasminnudāhṛtāḥ |
sāmañjasyādrateratra kintu tāḥ syuryathocitam || 15.58 ||
atha sādhāraṇaḥ
sādhāraṇaratiprāyaḥ sādhāraṇa itīritaḥ |
atra proktā vilāpāntāḥ ṣaḍdaśāntāśca komalāḥ || 15.59 ||
atha abhilāṣo, yathā prathamaskandhe
etāḥ paraṃ strītvamapāstapeśalaṃ
nirastaśaucaṃ bata sādhu kurvate |
yāsāṃ gṛhātpuṣkaralocanaḥ patir
na jātvapaityāhṛtibhirhṛdi spṛśan || 15.60 ||
cintādīnāṃ tathānyāsāmūhyā dhīrairudāhṛtiḥ || 15.61 ||
pūrvarāge prahīyeta kāmalekhasragādikam |
vayasyādikareṇātra kṛṣṇenāsya ca kāntayā || 15.62 ||
tatra kāmalekhaḥ
sa lekhaḥ kāmalekhaḥ syātyaḥ svapremaprakāśakaḥ |
yuvatyā yūni yūnā ca yuvatyāṃ saṃprahīyate || 15.63 ||
nirakṣaraḥ sākṣaraśca kāmalekho dvidhā bhavet || 15.64 ||
tatra nirakṣaraḥ
suraktapallavamayaścandrārdhādinakhāṅkabhāk |
varṇavinyāsarahito bhavedeṣa nirakṣaraḥ || 15.65 ||
yathā
kisalayaśikhare viśākhikāyā
nakharaśikhālikhito'yamardhacandraḥ |
dadhadiha madanārdhacandrabhāvaṃ
hṛdi mama hanta kathaṃ haṭhādviveśa || 15.66 ||
atha sākṣaram
gāthāmayī lipiryatra svahastāṅkaiṣa sākṣaraḥ || 15.67 ||
yathā jagannāthavallabhe
suiraṃ vijjhasi hiaaṃ lambhai maaṇo kkhu dujjasaḥ baliam |
dīsasi saaladisāsuṃ dīsai maaṇo ṇa kuttābi || 15.68 ||
suciraṃ bidhyasi hṛdayaṃ labhate madanaḥ khalu duryaśo balīyaḥ |
dṛśyase sakaladiśāsu dṛśyate madano na kutrāpi ||
bandho'bjatantunā rāgaḥ kiṃ vā kastūrikāmasī |
pṛthupuṣpadalaṃ patraḥ mudrākṛtkuṅkumairiha || 15.69 ||
atha mālyārpaṇam
suśliṣṭāṃ nijaśilpakauśalabharavyāhāriṇīmadbhutāṃ
goṣṭhādhīśvaranandanaṃ srajamimāṃ tubhyaṃ sakhi prāhiṇot |
ityākarṇya giraṃ saroruhadṛśaḥ svedodabindūcchalā
daṅgebhyaḥ kuladharmadhairyamabhitaḥ śaṅke bahirniryayau || 15.70 ||
kecittu
nayanaprītiḥ prathamaṃ cintāsaṅgastato'tha saṅkalpaḥ |
nidrācchedastanutā viṣayanivṛttistrapānāpa |
unmādo mūrcchā mṛtirityetāḥ smaradaśā daśaiva syuḥ || 15.71 ||
ityācakṣate |
evaṃ krameṇa vijñeyaḥ pūrvarāgo harerapi |
nidarśanāya tatraikamudāharaṇamucyate || 15.72 ||
yathā
upāraṃsīdvaṃśīkalaparimalollāsarabhasā
dvisasmāra sphārāṃ vividhakusumākalparacanām |
jahau kṛṣṇastṛṣṇāṃ sahacaracamūcārucarite
sakhi tvadbhrūvyālīculukitacalaccittapavanaḥ || 15.73 ||
iti pūrvarāgaḥ |
atha mānaḥ
dampatyorbhāva ekatra satorapyanuraktayoḥ |
svābhīṣṭāśleṣavīkṣādinirodhī māna ucyate || 15.74 ||
sañcāriṇo'tra nirvedaśaṅkāmarṣāḥ sacāpalāḥ |
garvāsūyāvahitthāśca glāniścintādayo'pyamī || 15.75 ||
ahetorneti netyukerhetoryanmāna ucyate |
asya praṇaya eva syānmānasya padamuttamam || 15.76 ||
tatra sahetuḥ
heturīrṣyāvipakṣādervaiśiṣṭye preyasā kṛte |
bhāvaḥ praṇayamukhyo'yamīṛṣāmānatvamṛcchati || 15.77 ||
tathā coktam
snehaṃ vinā bhayaṃ na syānnerṣyā ca praṇayaṃ vinā |
tasmānmānaprakāro'yaṃ dvayoḥ premaprakāśakaḥ || 15.78 ||
ataeva harivaṃśe
ruṣitāmiva tāṃ devīṃ snehātsaṅkalpayanniva |
bhītabhīto'tiśanakairviveśa yadunandanaḥ || 15.79 ||
rūpayauvanasampannā svasaubhāgyena garvitā |
abhimānavatī devī śrutvaiverṣyāvaśaṃ gatā || 15.80 ||
iti |
tatrāpi ca susakhyādi hṛdi yasyā virājate |
tasyā vipakṣavaiśiṣṭye na syādeva sahiṣṇutā || 15.81 ||
ataḥ satyāṃ vinānyāsāṃ susakhyāderabhāvataḥ |
śrute'pi pārijātasya dāne māno na cābhavat || 15.82 ||
śrutaṃ cānumitaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ tadvaiśiṣṭyaṃ tridhā matam || 15.83 ||
atha śravaṇam
śravaṇaṃ tu priyasakhīśukādīnāṃ mukhādbhavet || 15.84 ||
tatra sakhīmukhāt, yathā
śaśimukhi mṛṣā jalpaṃ śrutvā kaṭhorasakhīmukhāt
praṇayini harau mā viśrambhaṃ kṛthāḥ śithilaṃ vṛthā |
parihara manaḥklāntiṃ devi prasīda manorame
tava mukhamanālocya preyān vane'dya viśīryati || 15.85 ||
yathā vā
ahaha gahanā keyaṃ vārtā śrutau patitādya me
viditamanṛtaṃ hāsyādbrūṣe vimuñca kadarthanām |
sahacari kuto jīvatyasmin jane'pi janārdano
dyutaru kusumaṃ tasyai hā dhikkṛtī vitariṣyati || 15.86 ||
śukamukhā, yathā
āste kāciddayitakalahā krūracetāḥ sakhī te
kīro vanyaḥ sphuṭamiha yayā śyāmale pāṭhito'sti |
atha vyarthe vihagalapite suṣṭhu viśrambhamāṇā
mānārambhe na kuru hṛdayaṃ kātaro'smi prasīda || 15.87 ||
anumitiḥ
bhogāṅkagotraskhalanasvapnairanumitistridhā || 15.88 ||
atra bhogāṅkaḥ
bhogāṅko dṛśyate gātre vipakṣasya priyasya ca || 15.89 ||
tatra vipakṣagātre bhogāṅkadarśanaṃ, yathā
kālindītaṭadhūrta cāṭubhiralaṃ nidrātu candrāvalī
khinnākṣī kṣaṇamaṅganādapasara kruddhāsti vṛddhā gṛhe |
kiñcidbimbitadhātupatramakarīcitreṇa tatrādhunā
sarvā te lalitā lalāṭaphalakenodghāṭitā cāturī || 15.90 ||
priyagātre bhogāṅkadarśanaṃ, yathā vidagdhamādhave 
muktāntarnimiṣaṃ madīyapadavīmudvīkṣamāṇasya
jāne keśarareṇubhirnipatitaiḥ śoṇīkṛte locane |
śītaiḥ kānanavāyubhirviracito bimbādhare ca vraṇaḥ
saṅkocaṃ tyaja deva daivahatayā na tvaṃ mayā dūṣyase || 15.91 ||
tatra gotraskhalanam
vipakṣasaṃjñayāhvānamīrṣyātiśayakāraṇam |
āsāṃ tu gotraskhalanaṃ duḥkhadaṃ maraṇādapi || 15.92 ||
tena yathā bilvamaṅgale
rādhāmohanamandirādupagataścandrāvalīmūcivān
rādhe kṣemamiheti tasya vacanaṃ śrutvāha candrāvalī |
kaṃsa kṣemamaye vimugdhahṛdaye kaṃsaḥ kva dṛṣṭastvayā
rādhā kveti vilajjito natamukhaḥ smero hariḥ pātu vaḥ || 15.93 ||
yathā vā
ahaha vilasatyagre candrāvalī vimaladyutiḥ
kitava kalitā tārā sātra tvayā kva nu ṣoḍaśī |
timiramalinākāra kṣipraṃ vrajāruṇamaṇḍalā
mama sahacarī yāvanmanyudyutiṃ na vimuñcati || 15.94 ||
atha svapnaḥ
harervidūṣakasyāpi svapnaḥ svapnāyitaṃ mataḥ || 15.95 ||
tatra hareḥ svapnāyitam, yathā
śape tubhyaṃ rādhe tvamasi hṛdaye tvaṃ mama bahi
stvamagre tvaṃ pṛṣṭhe tvamiha bhavane tvaṃ girivane |
iti svapne jalpaṃ niśi niśamayantī madhuripo
rabhūttalpe candrāvaliratha parāvartitamukhī || 15.96 ||
vidūṣakasya, yathā
avañci caṭupāṭavairaghabhidādya padmāsakhī
tatastvaraya rādhikāṃ kimiti mādhavi dhyāyasi |
niśamya madhumaṅgalāditi giraṃ puraḥ svapnajāṃ
vidūnavadanā sakhi jvalati paśya candrāvalī || 15.97 ||
atha darśanam, yathā
mithyā mā vada kandare sama sakhīṃ hitvā tvamekākinīṃ
niṣkrāntaḥ pṛthusambhrameṇa kimapi prakhyāpayan kaitavam |
dūrātkiñcidañcitena rasanāśabdena sātaṅkayā
niṣkramyātha tayā śaṭhendra puline dṛṣṭo'si rādhāsakhaḥ || 15.98 ||
yathā vā
sahacari parigumphya prātarevārpitāsīd
brajapatisutakaṇṭhe yā mayotkaṇṭhayādya |
api hṛdi lalitāyāstasthuṣī hanta hṛnme
dahati dahanadīptiḥ paśya guñjāvalī sā || 15.99 ||
atha nirhetuḥ
akāraṇāddvayoreva kāraṇābhāsatastathā |
prodyan praṇaya evāyaṃ vrajennirhetumānatām || 15.100 ||
ādyaṃ mānaṃ parīṇāmaṃ praṇayasya jagurbudhāḥ |
dvitīyaṃ punarasyaiva vilāsabharavaibhavam |
budhaiḥ praṇayamānākhyaṃ eṣa eva prakīrtitaḥ || 15.101 ||
tathā coktam
aheriva gatiḥ premṇaḥ svabhāvakuṭilā bhavet |
ato hetorahetośca yūnormāna udañcati || 15.102 ||
avahitthādayo hyatra vijñeyā vyabhicāriṇaḥ || 15.103 ||
tatra kṛṣṇasya, yathā
avyaktasmitadṛṣṭimarpaya puraḥ svalpo'pi manturna me
patyurvañcanapāṭavādvrajapate jyotsnīniśārdhaṃ yayau |
śubhrālaṅkṛtibhirdrutaṃ pathi mayā dūraṃ tataḥ prasthite
sāndrā cāndramarundha bimbamacirādākasmikī kālikā || 15.104 ||
yathā vā
puṣpebhyaḥ spṛhayā vilambitavatīmālokya māmunmanāḥ
kaṃsāriḥ sakhi lambitānanaśaśī tūṣṇīṃ nikuñje sthitaḥ |
ātaṅkena mayā tadaṅghrinakhare kṣipte prasūnāñjalau
tasyālīkaruṣā bhruvaṃ vibhujato'pyāvirbabhūva smitam || 15.105 ||
kṛṣṇapriyāyāḥ, yathā uddhavasandeśe 
tiṣṭhan goṣṭhāṅgaṇabhuvi muhurlocanāntaṃ vidhatte
jātotkaṇṭhastava sakhi harirdehalīvedikāyām |
mithyāmānonnatikavalite kiṃ gavākṣārpitākṣī
svāntaṃ hanta glapayasi bahiḥ prīṇaya prāṇanātham || 15.106 ||
yathā vā
ahamiha vicinomi tvadgiraiva prasūnaṃ
kathaya kathamakāṇḍe caṇḍi vācaṃ yamāsi |
viditamupadhinālaṃ rādhike śādhi kena
priyasakhi kusumena śrotramuttaṃsayāmi || 15.107 ||
dvayoreva yugapad, yathā
kuñje tuṣṇīmasi nataśirāḥ kiṃ cirāttvaṃ murāre
kiṃ vā śyāme tvamapi vimukhī maunamudrāṃ tanoṣi |
jñātaṃ jñātaṃ smitavimuṣite kāpi vāmāsti yogyā
krīḍāvāde balavati yayā na dvayoreva bhaṅgaḥ || 15.108 ||
yathā vā
kuñjadvāri niviṣṭayostaraṇijātīre dvayoreva nau
tatrānyonyamapaśyatoḥ sakhi mudhā nirbandhataḥ klāntayoḥ |
haste drāgatha dāḍimīphalamabhinyaste mayā nistalaṃ
rādhāmudbhidurasmitāṃ parihasan phullāṅgamāliṅgiṣam || 15.109 ||
nirhetukaḥ svayaṃ śāmyetsvayaṃgrāhasmitāvidhiḥ || 15.110 ||
yathā
roṣastavābhūdyadi rādhike'dhika
stathāstu gaṇḍaḥ kathamucchvasityasau |
svanarmaṇetthaṃ durapahnavasmitāṃ
priyāmacumbatpaśupendranandanaḥ || 15.111 ||
heturyastu śamaṃ yathāyogyaṃ prakalpitaiḥ |
sāmabhedakriyādānanatyupekṣārasāntaraiḥ || 15.112 ||
mānopaśamanasyāṅkā bāṣpamokṣasmitādayaḥ || 15.113 ||
tatra sāma
priyavākyasya racanaṃ yattu tatsāma gīyate || 15.114 ||
yathā
jātaṃ sundari tathaymeva pṛthunā rādhe'parādhena me
kintu svārasiko mamātra śaraṇaṃ snehastvadīyo balī |
ityākarṇya giraṃ harernatamukhī bāṣpāmbhasāṃ dhārayā
sānaṅgotsavaraṅgamaṅgalaghaṭo pūrṇāvakārṣītkucau || 15.115 ||
atha bhedaḥ
bhedo dvidhā svayaṃ bhaṅgyā svamāhātmyaprakāśanam |
sakhyādibhirupālambhaprayogaśceti kīrtyate || 15.116 ||
tatra bhaṅgyā svamāhātmyaprakāśanaṃ, yathā vidagdhamādhave 
cañcanmīnavilocanāsi kamaṭhotakṛṣṭastanī saṅgatā
kroṅena sphuratā tavāyamadharaḥ prahlādasaṃvardhanaḥ |
madhyo'sau balibandhano mukharucā rāmāstvayā nirjitā
labdhā śrīghanatādya mānini manasyaṅgīkṛtā kalkitā || 15.117 ||
athavedaṃ priyoktitvātsāmodāharaṇaṃ bhavet |
nāyakasya svavacasā bhaṅgyāyaṃ bheda īryate || 15.118 ||
yathā
rūkṣā yanmayi vartase tvamabhitaḥ snigdhe'pi te dūṣaṇaṃ
tatrāste na hi kintu tatkila mamānaucityajātaṃ phalam |
yena svastaruṇīrupekṣya caramāmapyāśrayantīrdaśāṃ
premārtaṃ vrajayauvataṃ ca sumukhi tvaṃ kevalaṃ sevyase || 15.119 ||
sakhyādibhirupālambhaprayogo, yathā
kartuṃ sundari śaṅkhacūḍamathane nāsminnupekṣocitā
sarveṣāmabhayapradānapadavībaddhavrate preyasi |
ityālībhiralakṣitaṃ murabhidā bhadrāvalī bheditā
nāsāgre varamauktikaśriyamadhādasrasya sā bindunā || 15.120 ||
atha dānam
vyājena bhūṣaṇādīnāṃ pradānaṃ dānamucyate || 15.121 ||
yathā
kāmo nāma suhṛnmamāsti bhavatīmākarṇya matpreyasīṃ
hārastena tavārpito'yamurasi prāpnotu saṅgotsavam |
ityunnamya karaṃ muradviṣi vadatyudbhinnasāndrasmitā
padmā mānavinigrahātpraṇayinā tenodbhaṭaṃ cumbitā || 15.122 ||
atha natiḥ
kevalaṃ dainyamālambya pādapāto natirmatā || 15.123 ||
yathā
kṣitiluṭhitaśikhaṇḍāpīḍamārānmukunde
racayati ratikāntastomakānte praṇāmam |
nayanajaladharābhyāṃ kurvatī bāṣpavṛṣṭiṃ
varatanuriha mānagrīṣmanāśaṃ śaśaṃsa || 15.124 ||
atha upekṣā
sāmādau tu parikṣīṇe syādupekṣāvadhīraṇam |
upekṣā kathyate kaiścittūṣṇīmbhāvatayā sthitiḥ || 15.125 ||
taddvayaṃ, yathā
sūnurvallabha eṣa vallavapatestatrāpi vīrāgraṇī
statrāpi smaramaṇḍalīvijayinā rūpeṇa vibhrājitaḥ |
sakhyaḥ samprati rūkṣatā pṛthuriyaṃ tenātra na śreyase
dūre paśyata yāti niṣṭhuramanāḥ kā yuktiratrocitā || 15.126 ||
māne muhurnitibhirapyatidurnivāre
vācaṃyamavratamahaṃ tarasāgrahīṣam |
bāṣpaṃ tato vikiratī nijagāda padmā
pauṣpaṃ rajaḥ patitamatra dṛśormameti || 15.127 ||
athavā
prasādanavidhiṃ muktvā vākyairanyārthasūcakaiḥ |
prasādanaṃ mṛgākṣīṇāmupekṣeti smṛtā budhaiḥ || 15.128 ||
yathā
dhammille navamālatī paricitā savye ca śabdagrahe
mallī sundari dakṣiṇe tu kataratpuṣpaṃ tava bhrājate |
āghreyaṃ paricetumityupahite vyājena nāsāpuṭe
gaṇḍodyatpulakā vihasya hariṇā candrāvalī cumbitā || 15.129 ||
atha rasāntaram
ākasmikabhayādīnāṃ prastutiḥ syādrasāntaram |
yādṛcchikaṃ buddhipūrvamiti dvedhā taducyate || 15.130 ||
tatra yādṛcchikam
upasthitamakasmādyattadyādṛcchikamucyate || 15.131 ||
yathā
api gurubhirupāyairadya sāmādibhiryā
lavamapi na mṛgākṣī mānamudrāmabhāṅkṣīt |
harimiha parirebhe sā svayaṃgrāhamagre
navajaladharanādairbhīṣitā paśya bhadrā || 15.132 ||
yathā vā
upāyeṣu vyarthonnatiṣu bata sāmādiṣu sakhe
sakhīnāṃ cāturye gatavati ca sadyaḥ śithilatām |
viśākhāyāḥ kopajvaraharaṇamantrapratinidhiṃ
sacītkāraṃ rūkṣasvanitamakarodukṣadanujaḥ || 15.133 ||
atha buddhipūrvam
buddhipūrvaṃ tu kāntena pratyutpannadhiyā kṛtam || 15.134 ||
yathā
pāṇau pañcamukhena duṣṭakṛmiṇā daṣṭo'smi roṣāditi
vyājātkūṇitalocanaṃ vrajapatau vyābhujya vaktraṃ sthite |
sadyaḥ projjhitaroṣavṛttirasakṛtkiṃ vṛttamityākulā
jalpantī smitabandhurāsyamamunā gāndharvikā cumbitā || 15.135 ||
yathā vā
nyastaṃ dāma kṛtāgasādya hariṇā dṛṣṭvā puro rādhayā
kṣiptenābhihataḥ sa tena kapaṭī duḥkhīva bhugnānanaḥ |
mīlanneva niṣedivān bhuvi tataḥ sadyastayā vyagrayā
pāñibhyāṃ dhṛtakandharaḥ sthitamukho bimboṣṭhamasyāḥ papau
|| 15.136 ||
deśakālabalenaiva muralīśravaṇena ca |
vināpyupāyaṃ māno'sau līyate vrajasubhruvām || 15.137 ||
tatra deśabalena, yathā
alaṅkīrṇaṃ candrāvaliralighaṭājhaṅkṛtibharaiḥ
puro vṛndāraṇyaṃ kimapi kalayantī kusumitam |
hariṃ ca smerāsyaṃ priyakatarumūle priyamitaḥ
skhalanmānā sakhyāmadiśata satṛṣṇaṃ dṛśamasau || 15.138 ||
kālabalena, yathā
śaradi madhuramūrtiḥ paśya kānticchaṭābhiḥ
snapayati ravikanyātīravanyāṃ sudhāṃśuḥ |
iti niśi niśamayya vyāhṛtiṃ dūtikāyāḥ
smitarucibhiratānīttatra rādhā prasādam || 15.139 ||
muralīśabdena, yathā
yadi roṣaṃ na hi muñcasi
na muñca mama devi nātra nirbandhaḥ |
phutkṛtividhūtamānaḥ
sa bhavatu vijayī harerveṇuḥ || 15.140 ||
yathā vā
mānasyopādhyāyi prasīda sakhi rundhi me śrutidvandvam |
ayamuccāṭanamantraṃ siddho veṇurvane paṭhati || 15.141 ||
tāratamyaṃ tu mānasya hetoḥ syāttāratamyataḥ |
syāllaghurmadhyamaścāsau mahiṣṭhaścetyatastridhā || 15.142 ||
susādhyaḥ syāllaghurmāno yatnasādhyasya madhyamaḥ |
duḥsādhyaḥ syādupāyena mahiṣṭhaḥ preyasāpyayam || 15.143 ||
kṛṣṇe roṣoktayastāsāṃ vāmo durlīlaśekharaḥ |
kitavendro mahādhūrtaḥ kaṭhoro nirapatrapaḥ || 15.144 ||
atidurlalito gopībhujaṅgo ratahiṇḍakaḥ |
gopikādharmavidhvaṃsī gopasādhvīviḍambakaḥ || 15.145 ||
kāmukeśastamisraughaḥ śyāmātmāmbarataskaraḥ |
govardhanataṭāraṇyabāṭapāṭaccarādayaḥ || 15.146 ||
iti mānaḥ |
atha premavaicittyam
priyasya sannikarṣe'pi premonmādabhramādbhavet |
yā viśleṣadhiyārtistatpremavaicittyamucyate || 15.147 ||
yathā
ābhīrendrasute sphuratyapi purastīvrānurāgotthayā
viśleṣajvarasampadā vivaśadhīratyantamudghūrṇitā |
kāntaṃ me sakhi darśayeti daśanairudgūrṇaśasyāṅkurā
rādhā hanta tathā vyaceṣṭate yataḥ kṛṣṇo'pyabhūdvismitaḥ || 15.148 ||
yathā vā vidagdhamādhave 
samajani davādvitrastānāṃ kimārtaravo gavāṃ
mayi kimabhavadvaiguṇyaṃ vā niraṅkuśamīkṣitam |
vyaraci nibhṛtaṃ kiṃ vāhūtiḥ kayācidabhīṣṭayā
yadiha sahasā māmatyākṣīdvane vanajekṣaṇaḥ || 15.149 ||
vilāsamanurāgastu kutracitkamapi vrajam |
pārśve santamapi preṣṭhaṃ hāritaṃ kurute sphuṭam || 15.150 ||
suṣṭhūdāharatā paṭṭamahiṣīgītavibhramam |
spaṣṭaṃ muktāphale caitadvopadevena varṇitam || 15.151 ||
iti premavaicittyam
atha pravāsaḥ
pūrvasaṅgatayoryūnorbhaveddeśāntarādibhiḥ |
vyavadhānaṃ tu yatprājñaiḥ sa pravāsa itīryate || 15.152 ||
tajjanyavipralambho'yaṃ pravāsatvena kathyate |
harṣagarvamadavrīḍā varjayitvā samīritāḥ || 15.153 ||
śṛṅgārayogyāḥ sarve'pi pravāse vyabhicāriṇaḥ |
sa dvidhā buddhipūrvaḥ syāttathiavābuddhipūrvakaḥ || 15.154 ||
atra buddhipūrvaḥ
dūre kāryānurodhena gamaḥ syādbuddhipūrvakaḥ |
kāryaṃ kṛṣṇasya kathitaṃ svabhaktaprīṇanādikam || 15.155 ||
kiñciddūre sudūre ca gamanādapyayaṃ dvidhā || 15.156 ||
tatra ādyaḥ
dṛṣṭiṃ nidhāya surabhīnikurambavīthyāṃ
kṛṣṇeti varṇayugalābhyasena rasajñām |
śuśrūṣaṇe muralinisvanitasya karṇau
cittaṃ mukhe tava nayatyaharadya rādhā || 15.157 ||
atha dvitīyaḥ
bhāvī bhavaṃśca bhūtaśca tirvidhaḥ sa tu kīrtyate || 15.158 ||
tatra bhāvī, yathā uddhavasandeśe 
eṣa kṣattā vrajanarapateḥ ājñayā gokule asmin
bāle prāto nagaragataye ghoṣaṇāmātanoti |
duṣṭaṃ bhūyaḥ sphurati ca balādīkṣaṇaṃ dakṣiṇaṃ me
tena svāntaṃ sphuṭati caṭulaṃ hanta bhāvyaṃ na jāne || 15.159 ||
bhavan, yathā lalitamādhave 
bhānorbimbe tvaritamudayaprasthataḥ prasthite'sau
yātrānandīṃ paṭhati muditaḥ syandane gāndineyaḥ |
tāvattūrṇaṃ sphuṭa khurapuṭaiḥ kṣauṇipṛṣṭaṃ khananto
yāvannāmī hṛdaya bhavato ghoṭakāḥ sphoṭakā syuḥ || 15.160 ||
bhūto, yathā uddhavasandeśe 
kāmaṃ dūre sahacari varīvarti yatkaṃsavairī
na idaṃ lokottaramapi vipaddurdinaṃ me dunoti |
āśākīlo hṛdi kila vṛtaḥ prāṇarodhī tu yo me
so'yaṃ pīḍāṃ niviḍavaḍavāvahnitīvrastanoti || 15.161 ||
atra śrīyadusiṃhena preyasīhiramuṣya ca |
preṣaṇaṃ kriyate premṇā sandeśasya parasparam || 15.162 ||
yathā uddhavasandeśe 
soḍhavyaṃ te kathamapi balāccakṣuṣī mudrayitvā
tīvrottāpaṃ hatamanasijoddāmavikrāntacakram |
dvitraireva priyasakhi dinaiḥ sevyatāṃ devi śavye
yāsyāmi tvatpraṇayacaṭulabhrūyugāḍambarāṇām || 15.163 ||
tathā padyāvalyām 
kālindyāḥ pulinaṃ pradoṣamaruto ramyāḥ śaśaṅkāṃśavaḥ
santāpaṃ na harantu nāma nitarāṃ kurvanti kasmātpunaḥ |
sandiṣṭaṃ vrajayoṣitāmiti hareḥ saṃśṛṇvato'ntaḥpure
niḥśvāsāḥ prasṛtā jayanti ramaṇīsaubhāgyagarvacchidaḥ || 15.164 ||
atha abuddhipūrvaḥ
pāratntryodbhavo yastu proktaḥ so'buddhipūrvakaḥ |
divyādivyādijanitaṃ pāratantryamanekadhā || 15.165 ||
yathā lalitamādhave 
ānītāsi mayā manorathaśatavyagreṇa nirbandhataḥ
pūrṇaṃ śāradapurṇimāparimalairvṛndāṭavīmaṇḍalam |
sadyaḥ sundari śaṅkhacūḍakapaṭaprāptodayenādhunā
daivenādya virodhinā kathamitastvaṃ hanta dūrīkṛtā || 15.166 ||
cintātra jāgarodvegau tānavaṃ malināṅgatā |
pralāpo vyādhirunmādo moho mṛtyurdaśā daśa || 15.167 ||
tatra cintā, yathā haṃsadūte 
yadā yāto gopīhṛdayamadano nandasadanān
mukundo gāndinyāstanayamanuvindanmadhupurīm |
tadāmānkṣīccintāsariti ghanaghūrṇāparicayair
agādhāyāṃ bādhāmayapayasi rādhā virahiṇī || 15.168 ||
atha jāgaraḥ, yathā padyāvalyāṃ 
yāḥ paśyanti priyaṃ svapne dhanyāstāḥ sakhi yoṣitaḥ |
asmākaṃ tu gate kṛṣṇe gatā nidrāpi vairiṇī || 15.169 ||
atha udvegaḥ, yathā haṃsadūte 
mano me hā kaṣṭaṃ jvalati kimahaṃ hanta karavai
na pāraṃ nāvāraṃ kimapi kalayāmyasya jaladheḥ |
iyaṃ vande mūrdhnā sapadi tamupāyaṃ kathaya māṃ
parāmṛṣye yasmāddhṛtikaṇikayāpe kṣaṇikayā || 15.170 ||
atha tānavaṃ, yathā
udañcadvaktrāmbhoruhavikṛtirantaḥkaluṣitā
sadāhārābhāvaglapitakucakokā yadupate |
viśuṣyantī rādhā tava virahatāpādanudinaṃ
nidāghe kulyeva kraśimaparipākaṃ prathayati || 15.171 ||
atha malināṅgatā
himavisaraviśīrṇāmbhojatulyānanaśrīḥ
kharamarudaparajyadbandhujīvopamauṣṭhī |
aghahara śaradarkottāpitendīvarākṣī
tava virahavipattimlāpitāsīdviśākhā || 15.172 ||
atha pralāpaḥ, yathā lalitamādhave 
kva nandakulacandramāḥ kva śikhicandrakālaṅkṛtiḥ
kva mandramuralīravaḥ kva nu surendranīladyutiḥ
kva rāsarasatāṇḍavī kva sakhi jīvarakṣauṣadhir
nidhirmama suhṛttamaḥ kva bata hanta hā dhigvidhim || 15.173 ||
atha vyādhiḥ, yathā tatraiva 
uttāpī puṭapākato ‘pi garalagrāmādapi kṣobhaṇo
dambholerapi duḥsahaḥ kaṭuralaṃ hṛnmagnaśūlyādapi
tīvraḥ prauḍhavisūcikānicayato ‘pyuccairmamāyaṃ balī
marmāṇyadya bhinatti gokulapaterviśleṣajanmā jvaraḥ || 15.174 ||
atha unmādaḥ
bhramati bhavanagarbhe nirnimittaṃ hasantī
prathayati tava vārtāṃ cetanācetaneṣu |
lutḥti ca bhuvi rādhā kampitāṅgī murāre
viṣamavirahakhedodgārivibhrāntacittā || 15.175 ||
yathā vā
adyākāṇḍikamaṭṭahāsapaṭalaṃ nirmāti gharmāmbubhāk
cītkāraṃ kurute camatkṛtiparā sotkaṇṭhamākasmikam |
ākrandaṃ vitanoti ghargharaghanodghoṣaṃ kilātarkitaṃ
rādhā mādhavaviprayogarabhasādanyeva tīvrādabhūt || 15.176 ||
atha mohaḥ
nirundhe dainyābdhiṃ harati gurucintā paribhavaṃ
vilumpatyunmādaṃ sthagayati balādbāṣpalaharīm |
idānīṃ kaṃsāre kuvalayadṛśaḥ kevalamidaṃ
vidhatte sācivyaṃ tava virahamūrcchāsahacarī || 15.177 ||
atha mṛtyuḥ, yathā haṃsadūte 
aye rāsakrīḍārasika mama sakhyāṃ navanavā
purā baddhā yena praṇayalaharī hanta gahanā |
sa cenmuktāpekṣastvamapi dhigimāṃ tulaśakalaṃ
yadetasyā nāsānihitamidamadyāpi calati || 15.178 ||
pravāsavipralambhe'smin daśāstāstā harerapi |
atropalakṣaṇāyaikamudāharaṇamīryate || 15.179 ||
yathā
krīḍāratnagṛhe viḍambitapayaḥphenāvalīmārdane
talpe necchati kalpaśākhicamarīramye'pi rājñāṃ sutāḥ |
kintu dvāravatīpatirvrajagiridroṇīvilāntaḥśilā
paryaṅkopari rādhikāratikalāṃ dhyāyanmuhuḥ klāmyati || 15.180 ||
proktānāṃ premabhedānāṃ vividhatvāddaśā api |
vividhāḥ syurihetyetā bhūmabhītyā na kīrtitāḥ || 15.181 ||
etāstu premabhedānāmanubhāvatayā daśāḥ |
sādhāraṇyaḥ samastānāṃ prāyaśaḥ sambhavantyapi || 15.182 ||
kintvatraivādhirūḍhasya mohanatvamapeyuṣaḥ |
asādhāraṇarūpāstu tatprasaṅge puroditāḥ || 15.183 ||
vipralambhaṃ paraṃ kecitkaruṇābhidhamucire |
sa pravāsaviśeṣatvānnaivātra pṛthagīritaḥ || 15.184 ||
iti vipralambhabhedāḥ
atha saṃyogaviyogasthitiḥ
harerlīlāviśeṣasya prakaṭasyānusārataḥ |
varṇitā virahāvasthā goṣṭhavāmabhruvāmasau || 15.185 ||
vṛndāraṇye viharatā sadā rāsādivibhramaiḥ |
hariṇā vrajadevīnāṃ viraho'sti na karhicit || 15.186 ||
tathā ca pādme pātālakhaṇḍe mathurāmāhātmye
gogopagopikāsaṅge yatra krīḍati kaṃsahā || 15.187 ||
iti |
iti saṃyogaviyogasthitiḥ
atha sambhogaḥ
darśanāliṅganādīnāṃ ānukūlyānniṣevayā |
yūnorullāsamārohan bhāvaḥ sambhoga ucyate || 15.188 ||
manīṣibhirayaṃ mukho gauṇaśceti dvidhoditaḥ || 15.189 ||
tatra mukhyaḥ
mukhyo jāgradavasthāyāṃ sambhogaḥ sa caturvidhaḥ || 15.190 ||
tān pūrvarāgato mānātpravāsadvayataḥ kramāt |
jātān saṃkṣiptasaṅkīrṇasampannarddhimato viduḥ || 15.191 ||
tatra saṅkṣiptaḥ
yuvānau yatra saṃkṣiptān sādhvasavrīḍitādibhiḥ |
upacārānniṣevete sa saṃkṣipta itīritaḥ || 15.192 ||
tatra nāyakena kṛtaḥ, yathā saptaśatyām
līlāhituliaselo rakkhau vo rāhiātthaṇapphaṃse |
hariṇo paḍhamasamāgamasajjhasabebellio hattho || 15.193 ||
līlābhitulitaśailo rakṣatu vo rādhikāstanasparśe |
hareḥ prathamasamāgamasādhvasavevellito hastaḥ ||
nāyikāyāḥ, yathā
cumbe paṭāvṛtamukhī navasaṅgame'bhū
dāliṅgane kuṭilitāṅgalatā tadāsīt |
avyaktavāgajani kelikathāsu rādhā
modaṃ tathāpi vidadhe madhusūdanasya || 15.194 ||
atha saṅkīrṇaḥ
yatra saṅkīryamāṇāḥ syurvyalīkasmaraṇādibhiḥ |
upacārāḥ sa saṅkīrṇaḥ kiñcittaptekṣupeśalaḥ || 15.195 ||
yathā
sāsūyajalpitasudhāni samatsarāṇi
manoparāmaramaṇīyadṛgiṅgitāni |
kaṃsadviṣaḥ sphuradamandamukhānyanaṅga
vikrīḍitāni saha rādhikayā jayanti || 15.196 ||
yathā vā
vaktraṃ kiñcidavāñcitaṃ vivṛṇute nātiprasādodayaṃ
dṛṣṭirbhugnataṭā vyanakti śanakairīrṣyāvaśeṣacchaṭām |
rādhāyāḥ sakhi sūcayatyaviśadā vāgapyasūyākalāṃ
mānantaṃ bruvatī tathāpi madhurā kṛṣṇaṃ dhinotyākṛtiḥ || 15.197 ||
atha sampannaḥ
pravāsātsaṅgate kānte bhogaḥ sampanna īritaḥ |
dvidhā syādāgatiḥ prādurbhāvaśceti sa saṅgamaḥ || 15.198 ||
tatra āgatiḥ
laukikavyavahāreṇa syādāgamanamāgatiḥ || 15.199 ||
yathā uddhavasandeśe 
mā mandākṣaṃ kuru gurujanāddehalīṃ gehamadhyā
dehi klāntā divasamakhilaṃ hanta viśleṣato'si |
eṣa smero milati mṛdule vallavīcittahārī
hārī guñjāvalibhiralibhirlīḍhagandho mukundaḥ || 15.200 ||
atha prādurbhāvaḥ
preṣṭhānāṃ premasaṃrambhavihvalānāṃ puro hariḥ |
āvirbhavatyakasmādyatprādurbhāvaḥ sa ucyate || 15.201 ||
yathā śrīdaśame
tāsāmāvirabhūcchauriḥ smayamānamukhāmbujaḥ |
pītāmbaradharaḥ sragvī sākṣānmanmathamanmathaḥ || 15.202 ||
yathā vā haṃsadūte 
ayi svapno dūre viramatu samakṣaṃ śṛṇu haṭhā
daviśvastā mā bhūriha sakhi manovibhramadhiyā |
vayasyaste govardhanavipinamāsādya kutukā
dakāṇḍe yadbhūyaḥ smarakalahapāṇḍityamatanot || 15.203 ||
rūḍhākhyabhāvajāto'yaṃ sambhogo vaipralambhikaḥ |
nirbharānandapurāṇāṃ paramāvadhiriṣyate || 15.204 ||
dviguṇā virahārtiḥ syātsphuraṇe veṇurāgaje |
prādurbhāve bhavatyatra sarvābhīṣṭasukhotsavaḥ || 15.205 ||
atha samṛddhimān
durlabhālokayoryūnoḥ pāratantryādviyuktayoḥ |
upabhogātireko yaḥ kīrtyate sa samṛddhimān || 15.206 ||
yathā lalitamādhave 
dagdhaṃ hanta dadhānayā vapuridaṃ yasyāvalokāśayā
soḍhā marmavipāṭane paṭuriyaṃ pīḍātivṛṣṭirmayā |
kālindīyataṭīkuṭīrakuharakrīḍābhisāravratī
so'yaṃ jīvitabandhurinduvadane bhūyaḥ samāliṅgitaḥ || 15.207 ||
yathā vā 
tavātra parimṛgyatā kimapi lakṣma sākṣādiyaṃ
mayā tvamupasāditā nikhilalokalakṣmīrasi |
yathā jagati cañcatā caṇakamuṣṭisampattaye
janena patitā puraḥ kanakavṛṣṭirāsādyate || 15.208 ||
iti madhurarasaparipākavivekaḥ |
atha gauṇasambhogaḥ
channaprakāśabhedena kaiścideṣāṃ dvirūpatā |
iṣṭāpyatra na hi proktā nātyullāsakarī yataḥ || 15.209 ||
atha gauṇaḥ
svapne prāptiviśeṣo'sya harergauṇa itīryate |
svapno dvidhātra sāmānyaviśeṣatvena kīrtitaḥ || 15.210 ||
sāmānyaḥ sa tu yaḥ pūrvaṃ kathito vyabhicāriṣu |
viśeṣaḥ khalu jāgaryā nirviśeṣao mahādbhutaḥ || 15.211 ||
bhāvautkaṇṭhyemayo hyeṣa caturdhā pūrvavanmataḥ || 15.212 ||
tatra svapne saṅkṣipto, yathā
vihāraṃ kurvāṇastaraṇitanayātīravipine
navāmbhodaśreṇīmadhurimaviḍambidyutibharaḥ |
vidagdhānāṃ cūḍāmaṇiranudinaṃ cumbati mukhaṃ
mama svapne ko'pi priyasakhi balīyānnavayuvā || 15.213 ||
atha svapne saṅkīrṇo, yathā
sakhi kruddhā mā bhūrlaghurapi na doṣaḥ sumukhi me
na mānāgnijvālāmaśamayamahaṃ tāmasamaye |
sa dhūrtaste svapne rasavṛṣṭiṃ mayi tathā
yato vistīrṇāpi svayamiyamayāsīdupaśamam || 15.214 ||
atha svapne sampannaḥ, yathā haṃsadūte 
prayāto māṃ hitvā yadi kaṭhinacūḍāmaṇirasau
paryātu svacchandaṃ mama samayadharmaḥ kila gatiḥ |
idaṃ soḍhuṃ kā vā prabhavati yataḥ svapnakapaṭā
dihāyāto vṛndāvanabhuvi kalānmāṃ ramayati || 15.215 ||
atha svāpnasamṛddhimān, yathā lalitamādhave 
cirādadya svapne mama vividhayatnādupagate
prapede govindaḥ sakhi nayanayorakṣaṇabhuvam |
gṛhītvā hā hanta tvaritamatha tasminnapi rathaṃ
kathaṃ pratyāsannaḥ sa khalu puruṣo rājapuruṣaḥ || 15.216 ||
tulyasvarūpa evāyaṃ prodyan yūnordvayorapi |
ūṣāniruddhayoryadvatkvacitsvapno'pyabādhitaḥ || 15.217 ||
ataeva hi siddhānāṃ svapne'pi paramādbhute |
prāptāni maṇḍanādīni dṛśyante jāgare'pi ca || 15.218 ||
vyatītya turyāmapi saṃśritānāṃ
tāṃ pañcamīṃ premamayīmavasthām |
na sambhavatyeva haripriyāṇāṃ
svapno rajovṛttivijṛmbhito yaḥ || 15.219 ||
ityeṣa haribhāvasya vilāsaḥ ko'pi peśala |
citrasvapnamivātanvan kṛṣṇaḥ saṅgamayatyalam || 15.220 ||
athaiteṣu nirūpyante tadviśeṣāḥ supeśalāḥ |
ye'nubhāvadaśāmasyāḥ prāpnuvanti rateḥ sphuṭam || 15.221 ||
te tu sandarśanaṃ jalpaḥ sparśanaṃ vartmarodhanam |
rāsavṛndāvankrīḍāyamunādyambukelayaḥ || 15.222 ||
naukhelā līlayā cauryaṃ ghaṭṭaḥ kuñjādilīnatā |
madhupānaṃ vadhūveśadhṛtiḥ kapaṭasuptatā || 15.223 ||
dyūtakrīḍā paṭākṛṣṭiścumbāśleṣau nakhārpaṇam |
bimbādharasudhāpānaḥ samprayogādayo matāḥ || 15.224 ||
tatra sandarśanaṃ, yathā lalitamādhave 
calākṣigurulokataḥ sphurati tāvadantarbhayaṃ
kulasthitiralaṃ tu me manasi tāvadunmīlati |
calanmakarakuṇḍalasphuritaphullagaṇḍasthalaṃ
na yāvadaparokṣatāmidamapaiti vaktrāmbujam || 15.225 ||
atha jalpaḥ
jalpaḥ parasparaṃ goṣṭhī vitathoktiśca kathyate || 15.226 ||
atra parasparaḥ goṣṭhī, yathā dānakelikaumudyām
dharṣaṇe nakulastrīṇāṃ bhujaṅgeśaḥ kṣamaḥ katham |
yadetā daśanaireṣa daśannāpnoti maṅgalam || 15.227 ||
aprauḍhadvijarājarājadalikā labdhā vibhūtiṃ rucāṃ
navyāmātmani kṛṣṇavartmavilasaddṛṣṭirviśākhāñcitā |
kandarpasya vidagdhatāṃ vidadhati netrāñcalasya tviṣā
tvaṃ rādhe śivamūrtirityurasi māṃ bhogīndramaṅgīkuru || 15.228 ||
vitathoktiḥ, yathā tatraiva 
asminnadrau kati na hi mayā hanta hārādivittaṃ
hāraṃ hāraṃ hariṇanayanā grāhitā jainadīkṣām |
yāḥ kākūktisthagitavadanāḥ patradānena dīnās
tūrṇaṃ dūrādanujagṛhire prauḍhavallīsakhībhiḥ || 15.229 ||
atha sparśanam, yathā
na kuru śapathamasya sparśato dūṣitoccair
asi bhujabhujagena tvaṃ bhujaṅgādhipasya |
tanuranupamakampā svedamabhyudgirantī
kapaṭini paritaste paśya romāñcitāsti || 15.230 ||
atha vartmarodhanaṃ, yathā vidagdhamādhave 
parītaṃ śṛṅgeṇa sphuṭataraśilāśyāmalarucaṃ
valadvetraṃ vaṃśavyatikaralasanmekhalamamum |
atikramyottuṅgaṃ dharaṇidharamagre kathamitas
tvayā gantuṃ śakyā taraṇiduhitustīrasaraṇī || 15.231 ||
atha rāso, yathā
harirnavaghanākṛtiḥ prativadhūdvayaṃ madhyatas
tadaṃsavilasadbhujo bhramati citrameko'pyasau |
vadhūśca taḍidujjvalā pratiharidvayaṃ madhyataḥ
sakhīdhṛtakarāmbujā naṭati paśya rāsotsave || 15.232 ||
atha vṛndāvanakrīḍā
sthalakamalamalīnāṃ stauti gītaiḥ padaṃ te
radatatimatinamrā vandate kundarājī |
adharamanubhajantī lambate bimbamālā
vilasati tava vaśyā paśya vṛndāṭavīyam || 15.233 ||
yamunājalakeliḥ, yathā
vyātyukṣīyudhi rādhayā ghanarasaiḥ paryukṣyamāṇasya te
mālyaṃ bhaṅgamavāpa vīra tilako yātaḥ kilādṛśyatām |
vaktrendau pratimācchalena śaraṇaṃ labdhaḥ sakhīṃ kaustubhas
tanmā bhūścakito vimuktacikuraṃ nārdatyasau tvadvidham || 15.234 ||
yathā vā, padyāvalyāṃ 
jalakelitaralakaratala
muktapunaḥpihitarādhikāvadanaḥ |
jagadavatu kokayūnor
vighaṭanasaṅghaṭanakautukī kṛṣṇaḥ || 15.235 ||
atha naukhelā, yathā padyāvalyāṃ 
muktā taraṅganivahena pataṅgaputrī
navyā ca nauriti vacastava tathyameva |
śaṅkānidānamidameva mamātimātraṃ
tvaṃ cañcalo yadiha mādhava nāviko'si || 15.236 ||
atha līlācauryam
līlācauryaṃ bhavedvaṃśīvastrapuṣpādihāritā || 15.237 ||
atha vaṃśīcauryam, yathā padyāvalyāṃ 
nīcairnyāsādatha caraṇayornūpura mūkayantī
dhṛtvā dhṛtvā katakavalayānyutkṣipantī bhujānte |
mudrāmakṣṇoścakitaṃ śaśvadālokayantī
smitvā smitvā harati muralīmaṅkato mādhavasya || 15.238 ||
atha vastracauryaṃ, yathā
chadāvalivṛtaiva naḥ sapadi kācidekā vrajaṃ
praviśya jaratīrihānayatu ghorakarmoddhatāḥ |
ayaṃ guṇanidhistarorupari tābhirabhyarcyatām
umāvratakumārikāpaṭalacelapāṭaccaraḥ || 15.239 ||
atha puṣpacauryaṃ, yathā
ayi jñātaṃ jñātaṃ harasi hariṇākṣi pratidinaṃ
tvameva pracchannā mama sumanasāṃ mañjarimitaḥ |
cirāddiṣṭyā cauri tvamiha vidhṛtādya svayamato
guhākārāmārātpraviśa vasatiṃ prauḍhibhiralam || 15.240 ||
atha ghaṭṭaḥ, yathā dānakelikaumudyām 
ghaṭṭādhirājamavamatya vivādameva
yūyaṃ yadācaratha śulkamadityamānāḥ |
manye vidhitsatha tadatra girestaṭeṣu
durgeṣu hanta viṣameṣu raṇābhiyogam || 15.241 ||
atha kuñjādilīnatā, yathā vidagdhamādhave 
śaṅke saṅkulitāntarādya niviḍakrīḍānubandhecchayā
kuñje vañjulaśākhinaḥ śaśimukhī līnā varīvarti sā |
no cedeṣa tadaṅghrisaṅgamavinābhāvādakāle kathaṃ
puṣpāmodanimantritālipaṭalīstotrasya pātrībhavet || 15.242 ||
atha madhupānaṃ, yathā
mukhavidhumuditaṃ madhudviṣo'sau
madhucaṣake madhuraṃ samīkṣya mugdhā |
adiśata dṛśameva tatra pātuṃ
na tu vadanaṃ muhurarthitāpi tena || 15.243 ||
atha vadhūveśadhṛtiḥ, yathā uddhavasandeśe 
keyaṃ śyāmā sphurati sarale gopakanyā kimarthaṃ
prāptā sakhyaṃ tava mṛgayate nirmitāsau vayasyā |
āliṅgāmūṃ muhuriti tathā kurvatī māṃ viditvā
nārīveśaṃ hriyamupayayau māninī yatra rādhā || 15.244 ||
kapaṭasuptatā, yathā karṇāmṛte 
stokastokanirudhyamānamṛdulaprasyandimandasmitaṃ
premodbhedanirargalaprasṛmarapravyaktaromodgamam |
śrotuṃ śrotramanoharaṃ vrajavadhūlīlāmitho jalpitaṃ
mithyāsvāpamupāsmahe bhagavataḥ krīḍānimīladdṛśaḥ || 15.245 ||
atha dyūtakrīḍā
jitvā dyūtapaṇaṃ daśatyaghahare gaṇḍaṃ mudā dakṣiṇaṃ
sā vāmaṃ ca daśeti tatra rabhasādakṣaṃ kṣipantyabhyadhāt |
ājñāṃ sundari te yatheti hariṇā vāme ca daṣṭe tataḥ
saṃrambhādiva sā bhujālatikayā kaṇṭhe babandha priyam || 15.246 ||
atha paṭṭākṛṣṭiḥ, yathā lalitamādhave 
dhanyaḥ so'yaṃ maṇiraviraladhvāntapuñje nikuñje
smitvā smitvā mayi kucapaṭīṃ kṛṣṭavatyunmadena |
gāḍhaṃ gūḍhākṛtirapi tayā manmukhākutavedī
niṣṭhīvan yaḥ kiraṇalaharīṃ hrepayāmāsa rādhām || 15.247 ||
atha cumbo, yathā
kapaṭacaṭulitabhruvaḥ samantā
nmukhaśaśinaḥ rabhasādvidhūyamānam |
danujaripuracumbadambujākṣyāḥ
kamalamivānilakampi cañcarīkaḥ || 15.248 ||
atha āśleṣo, yathā
navajāguḍavarṇayopagūḍhaḥ
sphuradabhradyutiretayonmadena |
harati sma harirhiraṇyavallī
parivītāṅgatamālamaṅgalāni || 15.249 ||
atha nakhakṣataṃ, yathā
na kucāvimau gatijitā tayā hṛtaṃ
gajataḥ prasahya sakhi kumbhayoryugam |
kṣatamatra nāgadamano yadarpaya
tparamaṅgajāṅkuśavareṇa tatkṣaṇam || 15.250 ||
atha bimbādharasudhāpānaṃ, yathā
na hi sudhākarabimbasudhākaraṃ
kuru mukhaṃ karabhoru karāvṛtam |
adhararaṅgaṇamaṅga varāṅgane
pibatu nīpavanībhramarastava || 15.251 ||
atha samprayogo, yathā
drāgdormaṇḍalapīḍanoddhuradhiyaḥ proddāmavaijātyayā
nirbandhādadharāmṛtāni pibataḥ sītkārapūrṇāsyayā |
kandarpotsavapaṇḍitasya maṇiatirākrāntakuñjāntayā
sārdhaṃ rādhikayā harernidhuvanakrīḍāvidhirvardhate || 15.252 ||
vidagdhānāṃ mitho līlāvilāsena yathā sukham |
na tathā samprayogeṇa syādevaṃ rasikā viduḥ || 15.253 ||
yathā
balena parirambhaṇe nakhaśikhābhirullekhanaṃ
haṭhādadharakhaṇḍane bhujayugena bandhakriyām |
dukūladalane hatiḥ kuvalayena kurvāṇayā
ratādapi sukhaṃ hareradhikamādadhe rādhayā || 15.254 ||
yathā vā
narmotsekakalādṛgañcalacamatkārī bhruvorvibhramaḥ
saṃvyānasya vikarṣaṇcaṭulatāṃ karṇotpalenāhatiḥ |
krīḍeyaṃ vrajanāgarīratigurorgāndharvikāyāstathā
bhūyiṣṭhaṃ suratotsavādapi navāsvādaṃ vitene sukham || 15.255 ||
ataeva śrīgītagovinde 
pratyūhapulakāṅkureṇa niviḍaleśanimeṣeṇa ca
krīḍākūtavilokite'dharasudhāpāne kathākelibhiḥ |
ānandādhigamena manmathakalāyuddhe'pi yasminnabhud
udbhūtaḥ sa tayorbabhūva suratārambhaḥ priyambhāvukaḥ || 15.256 ||
yathā
gokulānanda govinda goṣṭhendrakulacandramaḥ |
prāṇeśa sundarottaṃsa nāgarāṇāṃ śikhāmaṇeḥ || 15.257 ||
vṛndāvanavidho goṣṭhayuvarāja manohara |
ityādyā vrajadevīnāṃ preyasī praṇayoktayaḥ || 15.258 ||
atulatvādapāratvādāpto'sau durvigāhatām |
spṛṣṭaḥ paraṃ taṭasthena rasābdhirmadhuro mayā ||o||
ayamujjvalanīlamaṇirgahanamahāghoṣasāgaraprabhavaḥ |
bhajatu tava makarakuṇḍalaparisarasevaucitīṃ deva ||o||
iti sambhogabhedāḥ |
iti śrīśrīujjvalanīlamaṇau śṛṅgārabhedaprakaraṇam |
sampūrṇo'yaṃ śrīśrīlarūpagosvāmi
prabhupādapraṇītaśrīśrīujjvalanīlamaṇirnāma granthaḥ |
